# Pedidos de arte: Japón y Gran Bretaña (293 pedidos)
# 47 leader_portrait, 101 event_picture, 59 national_spirit_icon, 74 weapon_icon, 12 plane_icon
# Cada pedido trae el nombre de archivo exacto: la imagen tiene que volver con ese nombre.
# El número es solo para ubicarse; se pueden mandar en cualquier orden y en varios zip.

## 1
ASSET_REQUEST
type: leader_portrait
id: ZJP_min_hoshino_1
filename: ZJP_min_hoshino_1.png
size: 156x210 (o más grande con la misma proporción)
style: photorealistic painted portrait matching the existing leader portraits of this Hearts of Iron IV mod: realistic skin pores, hair and fabric weave, subtle painterly softness, rich but natural colour grading, cinematic and believable near-future clothing of the year 2100 (not a sci-fi costume), no helmet or mask covering the face, no text, no letters, no numbers, no logos with words, no watermark, no frame, no border
description: WHO: Chamberlain Aiko Hoshino (Chambelana Aiko Hoshino), The Shipyard Shogunate, Japan risen against the High Seas Market Nation, year 2100. Known for: Chamberlain of Yokosuka. Lore: Era la secretaria del puerto de Yokosuka. La noche de la bandera vieja fue la que guardó los libros de cuentas en una caja fuerte y la llave en el bolsillo. Desde entonces el Shogunato le paga a quien ella dice. PERSON: a 42-year-old woman (Japanese): Japanese, light skin, black hair in a tight low bun with two lacquered pins, thin eyebrows, a small mole under the left eye. Expression: composed, attentive, the patience of someone who keeps every receipt. CLOTHING: a sharp dark suit with a high collar and a thin crimson tie, a small enamel rising-sun pin shaped like a crane hook on the lapel. DETAIL: a heavy brass key on a crimson cord around the neck, a thick leather ledger held closed against the chest with both hands. FRAMING: vertical 3:4 bust portrait (for example 624x840). The frame cuts at mid-chest; the top of the head sits about 7% below the top edge; the eyes are on the upper-third line; the person is centred and the shoulders fill about 70% of the width. Anything held stays inside the frame at chest height. CAMERA: eye level, 85mm portrait lens at f/2.8; the body turned about 25 degrees to the right, the face turned back almost to the camera, the eyes looking straight into the lens. LIGHT: soft key light from the upper left at 45 degrees, a gentle fill from the opposite side and a thin rim light separating the hair and shoulders from the background; cold floodlight from the docks with a crimson neon rim light. BACKGROUND: the harbour office of Yokosuka at dusk, filing cabinets and a window over the dry docks with cranes lit in crimson; recognisable but softer than the face (moderate depth of field), filling the space around the head and shoulders, in the colours of the nation.
transparent_background: false

style: Hearts of Iron IV style, retrofuturist year 2100, painterly propaganda-poster look, strong silhouette readable at small size, no text, no letters, no watermark; the Shipyard Shogunate of Japan: dry docks and giant cranes, welding sparks, mechas in scaffolding, old samurai banners over naval steel, neon city at night, navy steel, crane yellow and crimson
description: GENERATION 5 OF 5 of the Kamome Drone (Dron Kamome): a carrier-borne aircraft or drone with folding wings and an arrestor hook. It is the most advanced, final version of the same weapon line, with the SAME faction design language as generation 1 (arma_ZJP_carrier_plane.png): The Kamome Drone: a small carrier-borne drone with gull wings (kamome) that fold upward for storage, a white and pale grey body, a hazard-yellow arrestor hook, a single pusher propeller, a tiny crimson disc on each wing. Three-quarter view, wings half folded. Show the technological progress so it is obviously far newer than generation 1: each generation goes from propellers to early jets, swept wings, then stealthy delta shapes and finally an unmanned drone-like airframe. Keep the faction materials, colours and silhouette family; only the technology level changes, like Infantry Equipment I, II, III in Hearts of Iron IV. FACTION DESIGN PHILOSOPHY - BUILT IN THE DRY DOCKS. The Shipyard Shogunate of Japan makes every weapon in naval shipyards: hulls of riveted dark navy ship steel, crane hooks and winches as mounts, hazard-yellow stripes and numbers painted as shapes, welding seams left visible, and old samurai details grafted on top (horned crests, lacquered armour plates, small crimson rising-sun discs, silk cords). Machines walk more than they roll: the signature is the mecha, a two-legged war machine with a samurai silhouette. At night they glow with neon trim. Palette: dark navy steel, crane yellow, rust red, crimson, black lacquer, small neon accents. Single object, three-quarter or side view, centred, fills most of the frame, transparent background, no text, no letters, no numbers.
transparent_background: true
